# claude-plugins-official — Anthropic-curated plugin marketplace (snapshot 2026-05-21)

**Marketplace name:** `claude-community` (per `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`)
**Total plugins:** 1766
  - marketplace.json (community + first-party, pointer-style): 1715
  - In-repo Anthropic-managed under `plugins/`: 36
  - In-repo Anthropic-managed under `external_plugins/`: 15

**Source structure:** marketplace.json catalogues 1700+ community plugins by url/git-subdir reference. The 36 plugins in `plugins/` and 15 in `external_plugins/` are Anthropic-authored or Anthropic-vendored, loaded directly from this repo's directories and NOT listed in marketplace.json. Total = combined surface area users see when they install the claude-community marketplace.

**Categories** are explicit in marketplace.json for 41/1715 entries; the rest are inferred heuristically from name + description by this snapshot tool (not by the upstream). Categories are advisory only.

## Anthropic-managed plugins (in-repo under `plugins/`)
